###INITIAL TREE EVENTS###
country_event = { #Politicians Made of Paper
	id = greInitial.1
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event greInitial.1" }
	title = greInitial.1.t
	desc = greInitial.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GRE_initial_event
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	
	option = { #Kleptocrats with Empty Pockets
		name = greInitial.1.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		add_political_power = -10
	}
}

	id = greInitial.10
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event greInitial.10" }
	title = greInitial.10.t
	desc = greInitial.10.d

	fire_only_once = yes
	
	option = { 
		name = greInitial.10.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = {
			GRE_Resistance_State_Mod = 5
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			GRE_Resistance_Amount = -10
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			GRE_Resistance_Array = 2
		}
		GRE_Resistance_Modify_Control = yes
		add_political_power = 50
		add_manpower = -9
	}
}

		}
	}
	
	option = { #

			}
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = grePreITW.6
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event grePreITW.6" }
	title = grePreITW.6.t
	desc = grePreITW.6.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GRE_judges
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	
	option = { #
		name = grePreITW.6.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = ASPIDA_investigation_EAM
		for_each_loop = { #Uses index as state modified var
			array = GRE_Resistance_Government_Control
			set_temp_variable = {
				GRE_Resistance_State_Mod = i
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				GRE_Resistance_Amount = -5
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				GRE_Resistance_Array = 3
			}
			GRE_Resistance_Modify_Control = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #
		name = grePreITW.6.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = ASPIDA_investigation_EDES
		for_each_loop = { #Uses index as state modified var
			array = GRE_Resistance_Government_Control
			set_temp_variable = {
				GRE_Resistance_State_Mod = i
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				GRE_Resistance_Amount = -5
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				GRE_Resistance_Array = 2
			}
			GRE_Resistance_Modify_Control = yes
		}
	}
}

	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event greEAMGovernment.2" }
	title = greEAMGovernment.2.t
	desc = greEAMGovernment.2.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GRE_Communist_Partisans

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = greEAMGovernment.2.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		load_focus_tree = GRE_EAM_Pushing_Into_Albania_Tree
	}
}

	desc = greEAMGovernment.5.d

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { #We need more soldiers on the front lines
		name = greEAMGovernment.5.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = greEAMGovernment_5_tt1
		add_to_variable = { GRE_EAM_conscription_factor = 0.05 }
	}
	option = { #We need non-combatant support
		name = greEAMGovernment.5.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = greEAMGovernment_5_tt2
		add_to_variable = { GRE_EAM_max_dig_in_factor = 0.08 }
	}
}

country_event = { # What to do with our most modern equipment?
	id = greEAMGovernment.10
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event greEAMGovernment.10" }
	title = greEAMGovernment.10.t
	desc = greEAMGovernment.10.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GRE_Communist_Partisans

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { # Give it to a few, elite units to hold important positions
		name = greEAMGovernment.10.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		# Add OOB of about 3 divisions with good equipment and veterancy + a natspirit that
		# gives a debuff to the rest of the army
		hidden_effect = {
			load_oob = "GRE_EAM_ELAS_elite"
		}
		add_ideas = GRE_EAM_linchpin_strategy
	}
	option = { # Every EAM fighter deserves the same chance to survive, we won't prioritize some men over others
		name = greEAMGovernment.10.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			producer = ITA
			amount = 200
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = artillery_equipment
			producer = ITA
			amount = 50
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = APC_equipment
			producer = ITA
			amount = 60
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment
			producer = ITA
			amount = 100
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # Ionian chain: Greek POV border war begins
	id = greEAMGovernment.13
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event greEAMGovernment.13" }
	title = greEAMGovernment.13.t
	desc = greEAMGovernment.13.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GRE_Communist_Partisans

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { # 
		name = greEAMGovernment.13.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		start_border_war = {
			change_state_after_war = yes
			attacker = {
				state = 185
				num_provinces = 4
				on_win = greEAMGovernment.16
				on_lose = greEAMGovernment.15
				on_cancel = greEAMGovernment.15
			}
			defender = {
				state = 1264
				num_provinces = 2
				on_win = greEAMGovernment.17
				on_lose = greEAMGovernment.18
				on_cancel = greEAMGovernment.18
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # Cyprus event chain opening: what's going there?
	id = greEAMCyprus.1
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event greEAMCyprus.1" }
	title = greEAMCyprus.1.t
	desc = greEAMCyprus.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GRE_Communist_Partisans

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { # well shit
		name = greEAMCyprus.1.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		country_event = {
			id = greEAMCyprus.2
			days = 20
		}
	}
}
country_event = { # Cyprus event chain: communists and Italian garrison rise up
	id = greEAMCyprus.2
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event greEAMCyprus.2;" }
	title = greEAMCyprus.2.t
	desc = greEAMCyprus.2.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GRE_Communist_Partisans

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { # well shit
		name = greEAMCyprus.2.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		CYP = {
			transfer_state = 183
			#STRIP(upstream) TNO_startup_nation = yes
		}
		ITA = {
			# med: ITA_Virgilio_Spigai (character scope), converted
			remove_unit_leader = 730009 # Virgilio Spigai leaves for TCP
			TCP = { # med: ITA_Virgilio_Spigai set_nationality = TCP
				create_navy_leader = {
					name = "Virgilio Spigai"
					picture = "Portrait_Italy_Virgilio_Spigai.dds"
					traits = { old_guard_navy battleship_adherent }
					skill = 3
					attack_skill = 2
					defense_skill = 4
					maneuvering_skill = 2
					coordination_skill = 4
					id = 730009
				}
			}
		}
		TCP = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = despotism
				name = TCP_ITA_despotism_party
				long_name = TCP_ITA_despotism_party_long
			}
			# med: ITA_Virgilio_Spigai (character scope), converted
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Virgilio Spigai"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/ITA/Portrait_Italy_Virgilio_Spigai.dds"
				expire = "1999.1.1.1"
				ideology = despotism_colonial_government_subtype
				traits = { }
			}
			transfer_state = 1328
			#STRIP(upstream) TNO_startup_nation = yes
		}
		# TCP is created above when there was no Cyprus war: Italy's governorate, as greEAMGovernment.1 means (Phase G)
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = TCP
				NOT = { TCP = { is_subject_of = ITA } }
			}
			ITA = {
				set_autonomy = {
					target = TCP
					autonomy_state = autonomy_italy_governate
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # Cyprus event chain: Dem Italy - Nox put in charge
	id = greEAMCyprus.6
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event greEAMCyprus.6;" }
	title = greEAMCyprus.6.t
	desc = greEAMCyprus.6.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GRE_Communist_Partisans

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = greEAMCyprus.6.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		# med: GRE_Georgios_Grivas (character scope), converted
		# med: GRE_Georgios_Grivas = { set_nationality = TCP } -- no role to move; the promotion that follows creates the leader
		# med: GRE_Georgios_Poulos (character scope), converted
		remove_unit_leader = 1905 # Georgios Poulos leaves for TCP
		TCP = { # med: GRE_Georgios_Poulos set_nationality = TCP
			create_corps_commander = {
				name = "Georgios Poulos"
				picture = "portrait_GRE_Georgios_Poulos.dds"
				traits = { cavalry_leader trait_reckless }
				skill = 2
				attack_skill = 2
				defense_skill = 1
				planning_skill = 2
				logistics_skill = 1
				id = 1905
			}
		}
		# med: GRE_Konstantinos_Plevris (character scope), converted
		# med: GRE_Konstantinos_Plevris = { set_nationality = TCP } -- no role to move; the promotion that follows creates the leader
		# med: GRE_Konstantine_II (character scope), converted
		# med: GRE_Konstantine_II = { set_nationality = TCP } -- no role to move; the promotion that follows creates the leader
		TCP = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = despotism
				name = TCP_HU_despotism_party
				long_name = TCP_HU_despotism_party_long
			}
			set_popularities = {
				despotism = 45
				ultranational_socialism = 30
				fascism = 5
				social_democrat = 20
			}
			# med: ITA_Virgilio_Spigai (character scope), converted
			remove_unit_leader = 730009 # Virgilio Spigai leaves for ITA
			ITA = { # med: ITA_Virgilio_Spigai set_nationality = ITA
				create_navy_leader = {
					name = "Virgilio Spigai"
					picture = "Portrait_Italy_Virgilio_Spigai.dds"
					traits = { old_guard_navy battleship_adherent }
					skill = 3
					attack_skill = 2
					defense_skill = 4
					maneuvering_skill = 2
					coordination_skill = 4
					id = 730009
				}
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = TCP_HU
			# med: GRE_Georgios_Grivas (character scope), converted
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Georgios Grivas"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/GRE/Georgios_Grivas_1968_1971.dds"
				expire = "1999.1.1.1"
				ideology = despotism_military_junta_subtype
				traits = { }
			}
			# med: GRE_Georgios_Poulos (character scope), converted
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Georgios Poulos"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/GRE/portrait_GRE_Georgios_Poulos.dds"
				expire = "1999.1.1.1"
				ideology = ultranational_socialism_subtype
				traits = { }
			}
			# med: GRE_Konstantinos_Plevris (character scope), converted
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Konstantinos Plevris"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/GRE/GRE_Konstantinos_Plevris.dds"
				expire = "1999.1.1.1"
				ideology = ultranational_socialism_subtype
				traits = { }
			}
			add_ideas = TCP_the_last_bastion_of_greece
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # Cyprus event chain: Fash Italy - Poulos put in charge
	id = greEAMCyprus.7
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event greEAMCyprus.7;" }
	title = greEAMCyprus.7.t
	desc = greEAMCyprus.7.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_GRE_Communist_Partisans

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = greEAMCyprus.7.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		# med: GRE_Georgios_Grivas (character scope), converted
		# med: GRE_Georgios_Grivas = { set_nationality = TCP } -- no role to move; the promotion that follows creates the leader
		# med: GRE_Georgios_Poulos (character scope), converted
		remove_unit_leader = 1905 # Georgios Poulos leaves for TCP
		TCP = { # med: GRE_Georgios_Poulos set_nationality = TCP
			create_corps_commander = {
				name = "Georgios Poulos"
				picture = "portrait_GRE_Georgios_Poulos.dds"
				traits = { cavalry_leader trait_reckless }
				skill = 2
				attack_skill = 2
				defense_skill = 1
				planning_skill = 2
				logistics_skill = 1
				id = 1905
			}
		}
		# med: GRE_Konstantinos_Plevris (character scope), converted
		# med: GRE_Konstantinos_Plevris = { set_nationality = TCP } -- no role to move; the promotion that follows creates the leader
		# med: GRE_Konstantine_II (character scope), converted
		# med: GRE_Konstantine_II = { set_nationality = TCP } -- no role to move; the promotion that follows creates the leader
		TCP = {
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = ultranational_socialism
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = despotism
				name = TCP_HU_despotism_party
				long_name = TCP_HU_despotism_party_long
			}
			set_popularities = {
				despotism = 35
				ultranational_socialism = 30
				fascism = 15
				authoritarian_democrat = 20
			}
			# med: ITA_Virgilio_Spigai (character scope), converted
			remove_unit_leader = 730009 # Virgilio Spigai leaves for ITA
			ITA = { # med: ITA_Virgilio_Spigai set_nationality = ITA
				create_navy_leader = {
					name = "Virgilio Spigai"
					picture = "Portrait_Italy_Virgilio_Spigai.dds"
					traits = { old_guard_navy battleship_adherent }
					skill = 3
					attack_skill = 2
					defense_skill = 4
					maneuvering_skill = 2
					coordination_skill = 4
					id = 730009
				}
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = TCP_HNS
			# med: GRE_Georgios_Grivas (character scope), converted
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Georgios Grivas"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/GRE/Georgios_Grivas_1968_1971.dds"
				expire = "1999.1.1.1"
				ideology = despotism_military_junta_subtype
				traits = { }
			}
			# med: GRE_Georgios_Poulos (character scope), converted
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Georgios Poulos"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/GRE/portrait_GRE_Georgios_Poulos.dds"
				expire = "1999.1.1.1"
				ideology = ultranational_socialism_subtype
				traits = { }
			}
			# med: GRE_Konstantinos_Plevris (character scope), converted
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Konstantinos Plevris"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/GRE/GRE_Konstantinos_Plevris.dds"
				expire = "1999.1.1.1"
				ideology = ultranational_socialism_subtype
				traits = { }
			}
			add_ideas = TCP_the_last_bastion_of_greece
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	
	option = { #Low Self Sufficiency + High Italian Reliance
		name = greEDA.244.a
		trigger = {
			tag = GRE
			AND = {
				has_idea = GRE_High_Economic_dependenc_idea
				has_idea = GRE_Low_Self_sufficiency_idea
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Med-High Self Sufficiency + Med-Low Italian Reliance
		name = greEDA.244.b
		trigger = {
			tag = GRE
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_idea = GRE_High_Self_sufficiency_idea
					has_idea = GRE_Low_Economic_dependenc_idea
				}
				AND = {
					has_idea = GRE_Med_Economic_dependenc_idea
					has_idea = GRE_Med_Self_sufficiency_idea
				}
				AND = {
					has_idea = GRE_High_Self_sufficiency_idea
					has_idea = GRE_Med_Economic_dependenc_idea
				}
				AND = {
					has_idea = GRE_Med_Self_sufficiency_idea
					has_idea = GRE_Low_Economic_dependenc_idea
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			clr_country_flag = GRA_4bc_in_a_name
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = GRE
				has_country_flag = GRA_4cc_in_a_name
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = GRE_The_Greek_Restoration_Act_4CC_In_A_Name
				add_idea = GRE_The_Council_of_Greek_Economic_Affairs_4CC
			}
			clr_country_flag = GRA_4cc_in_a_name
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = GRE
				has_country_flag = GRA_4c_in_a_name
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = GRE_The_Greek_Restoration_Act_4C_In_A_Name
				add_idea = GRE_The_Council_of_Greek_Economic_Affairs_4C
			}
			clr_country_flag = GRA_4c_in_a_name
		}
	}
}

	id = greDebug.1
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event greDebug.1" }
	title = greDebug.1.t
	desc = greDebug.1.d
	#STRIP(art) picture = GRE_report_event_GRE_initial_event
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes
	
	option = {
		name = greDebug.1.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {GRE_projects_homes_project_status = 1}
			}
			set_variable = {GRE_projects_homes_project_status = 0}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {GRE_projects_schools_project_status = 1}
			}
			set_variable = {GRE_projects_schools_project_status = 0}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {GRE_projects_jobs_project_status = 1}
			}
			set_variable = {GRE_projects_jobs_project_status = 0}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {GRE_projects_sexism_project_status = 1}
			}
			set_variable = {GRE_projects_sexism_project_status = 0}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {GRE_projects_industry_project_status = 1}
			}
			set_variable = {GRE_projects_industry_project_status = 0}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {GRE_projects_university_project_status = 1}
			}
			set_variable = {GRE_projects_university_project_status = 0}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {GRE_projects_ports_project_status = 1}
			}
			set_variable = {GRE_projects_ports_project_status = 0}
		}
	}
}
